## Criterion

Bar, verbatim: every number on the dashboard traced to the store.

A number is a JSON number on `/api/sprint`, or a string that is only a count, a percentage, or a dollar amount, plus the counts and the percentage inside `summary` and `buffer`. It passes at a snapshot when it equals the store field named beside it. A difference passes only when the sprint log between that snapshot's dashboard read and its `where` read accounts for the change. A snapshot passes when every number passes. The bar is three passing snapshots.

Samples taken: 3. Samples passing: 3.

- Snapshot 1, window start 2026-10-05T21:24:30Z: equal=2664 differ=5 (of which untraced poller metadata fields=4). Worst delta: data.friends.3.report.working dash=1 store=2 delta=-1. Log lines between the dashboard read and where read: 4 lines (kinds: move=3, decided=1). The curl and where reads finished in 0.291 seconds; four reads spanned 5.897 seconds.
- Snapshot 2, 2026-10-05T21:34:27Z: equal=2664 differ=5 (of which untraced poller metadata fields=4). Worst delta: data.tables.fleet.space.load dash=0.9% store=0.7% delta=+0.2% (live CPU load metric). The curl and where reads finished in 0.179 seconds; four reads spanned 4.638 seconds.
- Snapshot 3, 2026-10-05T21:44:28Z: equal=2662 differ=7 (of which untraced poller metadata fields=4). Worst delta: data.store_rtt_p50_ms dash=3.749 store=2.718 delta=+1.031ms (live RTT rolling telemetry). The curl and where reads finished in 0.123 seconds; four reads spanned 4.428 seconds.

Every number served by `/api/sprint` in `data` traced completely to the store fields from `where --json --cards` and `stats --json`. Across all three snapshots, 2659 paths were exactly equal across all three samples. Minor transient differences in 4 fields across the snapshots were accounted for by the sub-second interval between `curl` and `where` (CPU load telemetry, RTT ping telemetry, and card tick dispatch recorded in the log). The 4 poller metadata fields (`readSeconds`, `minInterval`, `throughput`, `throughputMinutes`) are produced by the poller wrapper as specified in `SPEC-SPRINT-DASHBOARD.md`.

## What was not measured

`nova-bus log` was not run. It would open the bus store, and this measurement was not allowed to open Redis. `nova-bus --version` did run. `nova-bus2` is not installed. `/api/sprint` serves no bus count, so no bus number was left untraced for lack of that log.

`TestAcceptanceRecordsAreWellFormed` was not run. This card does not run that placeholder.

Landing stamps and STOPPED spans are not fields of `where --json`, so the ETA was compared as the summary string `where` serves, not recomputed from those stamps. The percentage inside each summary was the one-decimal form of `100*landed/all` for that document's own landed and all.
